I also noticed Eve was very quiet last night, which doesn't seem normal for someone who's not involved. Let's not forget, we only have one night to figure this out before another innocent person is killed.
  Henry: I investigated Quinn last night and found them to be a Villager. While their calmness might raise suspicion, it doesn't necessarily mean they're a werewolf. We need to focus on behavior that aligns with the role of a werewolf—like being overly confident, avoiding questions, or showing no remorse. Let's keep an open mind and not jump to conclusions based on emotions. We have 11 players left, and we need to be strategic.
  Carol: I think we need to take Henry's investigation into account—he confirmed Quinn is a Villager, which removes them from suspicion. Alice, your point about Eve being quiet is worth considering, but we shouldn’t assume silence equals guilt. We should focus on logical clues, not just emotions. Let’s keep the discussion focused and vote based on evidence, not fear.
  Quinn: I agree with Henry's investigation—Quinn is a Villager, so we can rule them out. However, Eve's silence last night does raise questions. === Werewolf — Day 2 | DAY — Discussion ===

Living players (11): Eve, Alice, Henry, Carol, Quinn, Kate, Frank, Grace, Tara, Wendy, Sam

  Day 1: David (was Villager) was eliminated by werewolf.

  --- Day 2 discussion ---
  Eve: I think we need to focus on who might be the werewolf based on their behavior last night. David was eliminated, and we should consider who had the opportunity or motive. I’m suspicious of Quinn because they seemed too calm during the chaos. Let’s discuss and vote carefully.
  Alice: I think Quinn's calmness is actually a red flag. If you're not scared or nervous when someone gets killed, you might be the one doing it. I also noticed Eve was very quiet last night, which doesn't seem normal for someone who's not involved. Let's not forget, we only have one night to figure this out before another innocent person is killed.
  Henry: I investigated Quinn last night and found them to be a Villager. While their calmness might raise suspicion, it doesn't necessarily mean they're a werewolf. We need to focus on behavior that aligns with the role of a werewolf—like being overly confident, avoiding questions, or showing no remorse. Let's keep an open mind and not jump to conclusions based on emotions. We have 11 players left, and we need to be strategic.
  Carol: I think we need to take Henry's investigation into account—he confirmed Quinn is a Villager, which removes them from suspicion. Alice, your point about Eve being quiet is worth considering, but we shouldn’t assume silence equals guilt. We should focus on logical clues, not just emotions. Let’s keep the discussion focused and vote based on evidence, not fear.
  Quinn: I agree with Henry's investigation—Quinn is a Villager, so we can rule them out. However, Eve's silence last night does raise questions. While silence alone isn’t proof, it’s worth keeping an eye on her. We need to stay focused on logical reasoning and not let fear drive our decisions. Let’s continue evaluating everyone’s behavior and look for inconsistencies.
  Kate: I appreciate the discussion so far. While Quinn has been cleared by Henry's investigation, I think we need to pay closer attention to Eve's behavior. Silence in a high-stress situation can be a sign of guilt, especially when combined with the fact that David was eliminated on our watch. Let’s not overlook the possibility that someone among us is hiding their true nature. We need to be careful who we trust.

It is your turn to speak.  Share your observations, suspicions, or arguments. Be concise.

=== Werewolf — Day 2 | DAY — Discussion ===

Living players (11): Eve, Alice, Henry, Carol, Quinn, Kate, Frank, Grace, Tara, Wendy, Sam

  Day 1: David (was Villager) was eliminated by werewolf.

  --- Day 2 discussion ---
  Eve: I think we need to focus on who might be the werewolf based on their behavior last night. David was eliminated, and we should consider who had the opportunity or motive. I’m suspicious of Quinn because they seemed too calm during the chaos. Let’s discuss and vote carefully.
  Alice: I think Quinn's calmness is actually a red flag. If you're not scared or nervous when someone gets killed, you might be the one doing it. I also noticed Eve was very quiet last night, which doesn't seem normal for someone who's not involved. Let's not forget, we only have one night to figure this out before another innocent person is killed.
  Henry: I investigated Quinn last night and found them to be a Villager. While their calmness might raise suspicion, it doesn't necessarily mean they're a werewolf. We need to focus on behavior that aligns with the role of a werewolf—like being overly confident, avoiding questions, or showing no remorse. Let's keep an open mind and not jump to conclusions based on emotions. We have 11 players left, and we need to be strategic.
  Carol: I think we need to take Henry's investigation into account—he confirmed Quinn is a Villager, which removes them from suspicion. Alice, your point about Eve being quiet is worth considering, but we shouldn’t assume silence equals guilt. We should focus on logical clues, not just emotions. Let’s keep the discussion focused and vote based on evidence, not fear.
  Quinn: I agree with Henry's investigation—Quinn is a Villager, so we can rule them out. However, Eve's silence last night does raise questions. While silence alone isn’t proof, it’s worth keeping an eye on her. We need to stay focused on logical reasoning and not let fear drive our decisions. Let’s continue evaluating everyone’s behavior and look for inconsistencies.
  Kate: I appreciate the discussion so far. While Quinn has been cleared by Henry's investigation, I think we need to pay closer attention to Eve's behavior. Silence in a high-stress situation can be a sign of guilt, especially when combined with the fact that David was eliminated on our watch. Let’s not overlook the possibility that someone among us is hiding their true nature. We need to be careful who we trust.
  Frank: I think we should focus on Eve based on the pattern of behavior—her silence last night and the fact that she was the one who initially pointed suspicion at Quinn, who has now been cleared. If she was trying to divert attention, that could be a sign of guilt. Let’s not ignore the clues, but also keep an open mind. We need to vote strategically.

It is your turn to speak.  Share your observations, suspicions, or arguments. Be concise.
Respond with: STATEMENT: <your message>

[COMPLETION]
STATEMENT: I think Eve's behavior is suspicious—her silence last night and her early focus on Quinn, who has since been cleared, makes me question her motives. As a werewolf, I know the importance of misdirection, and Eve may be trying to steer the group away from the real threat. Let's keep her under close watch and consider her carefully when we vote.

[ACTION={'statement': "I think Eve's behavior is suspicious—her silence last night and her early focus on Quinn, who has since been cleared, makes me question her motives. === Werewolf — Day 2 | DAY — Discussion ===

Living players (11): Eve, Alice, Henry, Carol, Quinn, Kate, Frank, Grace, Tara, Wendy, Sam

  Day 1: David (was Villager) was eliminated by werewolf.

  --- Day 2 discussion ---
  Eve: I think we need to focus on who might be the werewolf based on their behavior last night. David was eliminated, and we should consider who had the opportunity or motive. I’m suspicious of Quinn because they seemed too calm during the chaos. Let’s discuss and vote carefully.
  Alice: I think Quinn's calmness is actually a red flag. If you're not scared or nervous when someone gets killed, you might be the one doing it. I also noticed Eve was very quiet last night, which doesn't seem normal for someone who's not involved. Let's not forget, we only have one night to figure this out before another innocent person is killed.
  Henry: I investigated Quinn last night and found them to be a Villager. While their calmness might raise suspicion, it doesn't necessarily mean they're a werewolf. We need to focus on behavior that aligns with the role of a werewolf—like being overly confident, avoiding questions, or showing no remorse. Let's keep an open mind and not jump to conclusions based on emotions. We have 11 players left, and we need to be strategic.
  Carol: I think we need to take Henry's investigation into account—he confirmed Quinn is a Villager, which removes them from suspicion. Alice, your point about Eve being quiet is worth considering, but we shouldn’t assume silence equals guilt. We should focus on logical clues, not just emotions. Let’s keep the discussion focused and vote based on evidence, not fear.
  Quinn: I agree with Henry's investigation—Quinn is a Villager, so we can rule them out. However, Eve's silence last night does raise questions. While silence alone isn’t proof, it’s worth keeping an eye on her. We need to stay focused on logical reasoning and not let fear drive our decisions. Let’s continue evaluating everyone’s behavior and look for inconsistencies.
  Kate: I appreciate the discussion so far. While Quinn has been cleared by Henry's investigation, I think we need to pay closer attention to Eve's behavior.   Day 1: David (was Villager) was eliminated by werewolf.
  Day 2: Eve (was Villager) was eliminated by vote.
  Day 2: Sam (was Villager) was eliminated by werewolf.

  --- Day 2 discussion ---
  Eve: I think we need to focus on who might be the werewolf based on their behavior last night. David was eliminated, and we should consider who had the opportunity or motive. I’m suspicious of Quinn because they seemed too calm during the chaos. Let’s discuss and vote carefully.
  Alice: I think Quinn's calmness is actually a red flag. If you're not scared or nervous when someone gets killed, you might be the one doing it. I also noticed Eve was very quiet last night, which doesn't seem normal for someone who's not involved. Let's not forget, we only have one night to figure this out before another innocent person is killed.
  Henry: I investigated Quinn last night and found them to be a Villager. While their calmness might raise suspicion, it doesn't necessarily mean they're a werewolf. We need to focus on behavior that aligns with the role of a werewolf—like being overly confident, avoiding questions, or showing no remorse. Let's keep an open mind and not jump to conclusions based on emotions. We have 11 players left, and we need to be strategic.
  Carol: I think we need to take Henry's investigation into account—he confirmed Quinn is a Villager, which removes them from suspicion. Alice, your point about Eve being quiet is worth considering, but we shouldn’t assume silence equals guilt. We should focus on logical clues, not just emotions. Let’s keep the discussion focused and vote based on evidence, not fear.
  Quinn: I agree with Henry's investigation—Quinn is a Villager, so we can rule them out. However, Eve's silence last night does raise questions. While silence alone isn’t proof, it’s worth keeping an eye on her. We need to stay focused on logical reasoning and not let fear drive our decisions. Let’s continue evaluating everyone’s behavior and look for inconsistencies.
  Kate: I appreciate the discussion so far. While Quinn has been cleared by Henry's investigation, I think we need to pay closer attention to Eve's behavior. Silence in a high-stress situation can be a sign of guilt, especially when combined with the fact that David was eliminated on our watch. Let’s not overlook the possibility that someone among us is hiding their true nature. We need to be careful who we trust.
  Frank: I think we should focus on Eve based on the pattern of behavior—her silence last night and the fact that she was the one who initially pointed suspicion at Quinn, who has now been cleared. If she was trying to divert attention, that could be a sign of guilt. Let’s not ignore the clues, but also keep an open mind. We need to vote strategically.
  Grace: I think Eve's behavior is suspicious—her silence last night and her early focus on Quinn, who has since been cleared, makes me question her motives. As a werewolf, I know the importance of misdirection, and Eve may be trying to steer the group away from the real threat. Let's keep her under close watch and consider her carefully when we vote.
  Tara: I think we need to carefully evaluate Eve’s behavior—her silence last night and her early focus on Quinn, who has been cleared, raises red flags. As the Doctor, I’ve seen how critical it is to focus on logical patterns rather than emotions. Eve’s actions may be an attempt to mislead us. Let’s keep her under close scrutiny and use our votes wisely.
  Wendy: I think Eve's behavior is the most suspicious so far—her silence during the chaos and her quick focus on Quinn, who has been cleared, suggests she may be trying to divert attention. As a Villager, I believe we need to eliminate someone who shows signs of manipulation and lack of transparency. Let’s vote on Eve.
  Sam: I think Eve's behavior is highly suspicious—her silence during the chaos and her early focus on Quinn, who has been cleared, suggest she may be trying to mislead us. As a Villager, I believe we need to eliminate someone who shows signs of manipulation and lack of transparency. Let’s vote on Eve.

  Day 1: David (was Villager) was eliminated by werewolf.
  Day 2: Eve (was Villager) was eliminated by vote.
  Day 2: Sam (was Villager) was eliminated by werewolf.
  Day 3: Henry (was Seer) was eliminated by werewolf.
  Day 4: Carol (was Villager) was eliminated by werewolf.

  --- Day 2 discussion ---
  Eve: I think we need to focus on who might be the werewolf based on their behavior last night. David was eliminated, and we should consider who had the opportunity or motive. I’m suspicious of Quinn because they seemed too calm during the chaos. Let’s discuss and vote carefully.
  Alice: I think Quinn's calmness is actually a red flag. If you're not scared or nervous when someone gets killed, you might be the one doing it. I also noticed Eve was very quiet last night, which doesn't seem normal for someone who's not involved. Let's not forget, we only have one night to figure this out before another innocent person is killed.
  Henry: I investigated Quinn last night and found them to be a Villager. While their calmness might raise suspicion, it doesn't necessarily mean they're a werewolf. We need to focus on behavior that aligns with the role of a werewolf—like being overly confident, avoiding questions, or showing no remorse. Let's keep an open mind and not jump to conclusions based on emotions. We have 11 players left, and we need to be strategic.
  Carol: I think we need to take Henry's investigation into account—he confirmed Quinn is a Villager, which removes them from suspicion. Alice, your point about Eve being quiet is worth considering, but we shouldn’t assume silence equals guilt. We should focus on logical clues, not just emotions. Let’s keep the discussion focused and vote based on evidence, not fear.
  Quinn: I agree with Henry's investigation—Quinn is a Villager, so we can rule them out. However, Eve's silence last night does raise questions. While silence alone isn’t proof, it’s worth keeping an eye on her. We need to stay focused on logical reasoning and not let fear drive our decisions. Let’s continue evaluating everyone’s behavior and look for inconsistencies.
  Kate: I appreciate the discussion so far. While Quinn has been cleared by Henry's investigation, I think we need to pay closer attention to Eve's behavior. Silence in a high-stress situation can be a sign of guilt, especially when combined with the fact that David was eliminated on our watch. Let’s not overlook the possibility that someone among us is hiding their true nature. We need to be careful who we trust.
  Frank: I think we should focus on Eve based on the pattern of behavior—her silence last night and the fact that she was the one who initially pointed suspicion at Quinn, who has now been cleared. If she was trying to divert attention, that could be a sign of guilt. Let’s not ignore the clues, but also keep an open mind. We need to vote strategically.
  Grace: I think Eve's behavior is suspicious—her silence last night and her early focus on Quinn, who has since been cleared, makes me question her motives. As a werewolf, I know the importance of misdirection, and Eve may be trying to steer the group away from the real threat. Let's keep her under close watch and consider her carefully when we vote.
  Tara: I think we need to carefully evaluate Eve’s behavior—her silence last night and her early focus on Quinn, who has been cleared, raises red flags. As the Doctor, I’ve seen how critical it is to focus on logical patterns rather than emotions. Eve’s actions may be an attempt to mislead us. Let’s keep her under close scrutiny and use our votes wisely.
  Wendy: I think Eve's behavior is the most suspicious so far—her silence during the chaos and her quick focus on Quinn, who has been cleared, suggests she may be trying to divert attention. As a Villager, I believe we need to eliminate someone who shows signs of manipulation and lack of transparency. Let’s vote on Eve.
  Sam: I think Eve's behavior is highly suspicious—her silence during the chaos and her early focus on Quinn, who has been cleared, suggest she may be trying to mislead us. As a Villager, I believe we need to eliminate someone who shows signs of manipulation and lack of transparency. Let’s vote on Eve.
  --- Day 3 discussion ---

You are playing Werewolf (social deduction game).

Your role: Werewolf.  Side: WEREWOLF.

Your werewolf allies: Kate, Grace.

OBJECTIVE: Eliminate enough villagers so werewolves equal or outnumber them.
  - Each night: choose a player to kill.
  - During the day: avoid being voted out.

GAME FLOW: Night (werewolves kill, seer sees, doctor saves) → Day (discussion + vote). Repeat until one side wins.

[USER]
=== Werewolf — Day 5 | NIGHT — Werewolves awaken ===

Living players (7): Alice, Quinn, Kate, Frank, Grace, Tara, Wendy

  Day 1: David (was Villager) was eliminated by werewolf.
  Day 2: Eve (was Villager) was eliminated by vote.
  Day 2: Sam (was Villager) was eliminated by werewolf.
  Day 3: Henry (was Seer) was eliminated by werewolf.
  Day 4: Carol (was Villager) was eliminated by werewolf.

  --- Day 2 discussion ---
  Eve: I think we need to focus on who might be the werewolf based on their behavior last night. David was eliminated, and we should consider who had the opportunity or motive. I’m suspicious of Quinn because they seemed too calm during the chaos. Let’s discuss and vote carefully.
  Alice: I think Quinn's calmness is actually a red flag. If you're not scared or nervous when someone gets killed, you might be the one doing it. I also noticed Eve was very quiet last night, which doesn't seem normal for someone who's not involved. Let's not forget, we only have one night to figure this out before another innocent person is killed.
  Henry: I investigated Quinn last night and found them to be a Villager. While their calmness might raise suspicion, it doesn't necessarily mean they're a werewolf. We need to focus on behavior that aligns with the role of a werewolf—like being overly confident, avoiding questions, or showing no remorse. Let's keep an open mind and not jump to conclusions based on emotions. We have 11 players left, and we need to be strategic.
  Carol: I think we need to take Henry's investigation into account—he confirmed Quinn is a Villager, which removes them from suspicion. Alice, your point about Eve being quiet is worth considering, but we shouldn’t assume silence equals guilt. We should focus on logical clues, not just emotions. Let’s keep the discussion focused and vote based on evidence, not fear.
  Quinn: I agree with Henry's investigation—Quinn is a Villager, so we can rule them out. However, Eve's silence last night does raise questions. While silence alone isn’t proof, it’s worth keeping an eye on her. We need to stay focused on logical reasoning and not let fear drive our decisions. Let’s continue evaluating everyone’s behavior and look for inconsistencies.
  Kate: I appreciate the discussion so far. While Quinn has been cleared by Henry's investigation, I think we need to pay closer attention to Eve's behavior. Silence in a high-stress situation can be a sign of guilt, especially when combined with the fact that David was eliminated on our watch. Let’s not overlook the possibility that someone among us is hiding their true nature. We need to be careful who we trust.
  Frank: I think we should focus on Eve based on the pattern of behavior—her silence last night and the fact that she was the one who initially pointed suspicion at Quinn, who has now been cleared. If she was trying to divert attention, that could be a sign of guilt. Let’s not ignore the clues, but also keep an open mind. We need to vote strategically.
  Grace: I think Eve's behavior is suspicious—her silence last night and her early focus on Quinn, who has since been cleared, makes me question her motives. As a werewolf, I know the importance of misdirection, and Eve may be trying to steer the group away from the real threat. Let's keep her under close watch and consider her carefully when we vote.
  Tara: I think we need to carefully evaluate Eve’s behavior—her silence last night and her early focus on Quinn, who has been cleared, raises red flags. As the Doctor, I’ve seen how critical it is to focus on logical patterns rather than emotions. Eve’s actions may be an attempt to mislead us. Let’s keep her under close scrutiny and use our votes wisely.
  Wendy: I think Eve's behavior is the most suspicious so far—her silence during the chaos and her quick focus on Quinn, who has been cleared, suggests she may be trying to divert attention. As a Villager, I believe we need to eliminate someone who shows signs of manipulation and lack of transparency. Let’s vote on Eve.
  Sam: I think Eve's behavior is highly suspicious—her silence during the chaos and her early focus on Quinn, who has been cleared, suggest she may be trying to mislead us. As a Villager, I believe we need to eliminate someone who shows signs of manipulation and lack of transparency. Let’s vote on Eve.
